# jsaddle-dom / ghcjs-dom 0.10 API changes

Generated by `dom-gen apidiff` from the 0.9.9.3 baseline. Inputs: webref and browser-compat-data as pinned in `dom-gen/inputs.nix`.

## Removed JavaScript members

Members of the old bindings that the new ones do not bind, by interface.
*not in specs*: no current specification defines it (often WebKit-only or
removed from the web platform). *support*: it is in a specification but
ships in fewer than two engines (the engines listed have it).

| | interfaces | members |
|---|---:|---:|
| interface not in specs | 158 | 796 |
| interface filtered by support | 0 | 0 |
| member not in specs | | 375 |
| member filtered by support | | 2 |
